{"forms":[{"form":"enterers","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"1585, Arthur Golding (translator), The Worke of Pomponius Mela, the Cosmographer, concerninge the Situation of the World, London: Thomas Hacket, Book 1, Chapter 13, “Of Cilicia,” p. 23,\nWhen ye come to the bottome, there againe openeth an other Caue, woorthy to be spoken of for other things.
